//! Column worlds execute authored observers, including derived/count/refusal paths.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#121-column-read-intent — excluding a field from a projection must not produce a synthetic whole-row refutation.
use super::*;
fn discharged(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->bool{
    inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::implies(&super::super::columns::admission(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],inv.column.as_deref()),p)==Ok(true))
        &&!bound.schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.source==inv.table)
        &&!bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(&inv.table)))
        &&!bound.programs.iter().any(|p|ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(t,_)|t!=&inv.table)&&super::super::audit::mentions(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),&inv.table))
}
fn alternative(kind:&C,old:&Value)->Option<Value>{Some(match (kind.required(),old){
    (C::Text{max},Value::String(s)) if s.is_empty()=>if *max>0{Value::String("x".into())}else{return None},
    (C::Text{..},Value::String(s))=>Value::String(s.chars().map(|c|if c=='y'{'x'}else{'y'}).collect()),
    (C::Enum{variants},_)=>Value::String(variants.iter().find(|s|Value::String((*s).clone())!=*old)?.clone()),
    (C::Principal,Value::Id(s))=>Value::Id(if s==VIEWER{OTHER}else{VIEWER}.into()),
    (C::Int|C::Time,Value::Int(n))=>Value::Int(n.checked_add(1)?),
    (C::IntRange{min,max},Value::Int(n))=>Value::Int(if n<max{n+1}else if n>min{n-1}else{return None}),
    (C::Bool,Value::Bool(v))=>Value::Bool(!v),
    _=>return None,
})}
fn sample_rows(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,inv:&Invariant,row:&Value,presence:bool)->Option<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>>{
    let column=inv.column.as_ref()?;let kind=bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].columns.get(column)?;
    let old=row.field(column).ok()?;
    let alt=if presence&&kind.is_optional(){if *old==Value::Null{sample(kind.required(),0)?}else{Value::Null}}else{alternative(kind,old)?};
    let mut out=rows.clone();
    let selected=out.get_mut(&inv.table)?.iter_mut().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok())?;
    if let Value::Row(fields)=selected{fields.insert(column.clone(),alt);}else{return None;}
    Some(out)
}
fn run_pair_variant(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,row:&Value,fixture:Option<&Engine>,trace:Option<(&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,&[histories::Step])>,presence:bool,out:&mut Campaign){
    if out.column_observer_calls>=16384{out.unsupported.insert("column observers capped at 16384 calls; remaining value worlds not covered".into());return;}
    if discharged(bound,inv){return;}
    let Some(other)=sample_rows(bound,rows,inv,row,presence)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: column pair needs a second typed non-asset value",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap_or("")));return;};
    let altrow=other[&inv.table].iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()).unwrap();
    let mut actors=histories::viewers(bound);
    for (table,rs) in rows{for r in rs{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=r.field(c)&&!actors.contains(p){actors.push(p.clone());}}}}
    'actors: for viewer in &actors{
        let committed=trace.map(|(_,steps)|steps.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count()).unwrap_or(0);
        if fixture.is_some()&&out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==*viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&v.history.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count()<=committed){continue;}

        {
            let judge=if let Some(fixture)=fixture{fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())}else{engine(bound,rows,program("$column.intent",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]))};
            let Ok(mut judge)=judge else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: primary column intent world unavailable",inv.target_label()));continue;};
            let actual=match readback(&judge){Ok(rows)=>rows,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(why);continue;}};
            let Some(primary)=actual.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok())else{continue;};
            if policy_in(bound,inv,&actual,viewer,None,primary,&mut judge)!=Ok(false){continue;}
        }
        for bp in &bound.programs{
            let p=&bp.program;
            if !matches!(p.kind,ProgramKind::Query|ProgramKind::Mutation)||p.native{continue;}
            let json=serde_json::to_string(p).unwrap();
            if ["NewId","Emit","JobAs","JobQuery"].iter().any(|s|json.contains(s)){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated-id/opaque column observer declined",p.name));continue;}
            if p.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation {
                if inv.policy.is_none(){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column mutation has unknown policy dependencies; disclosure-boundary tracing required",p.name));continue;}
                let writes=ir::write_tables(p);
                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(|r|stability::dependencies(bound,r)).unwrap_or_default();
                if writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table||deps.contains(t)){
                    out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column mutation can change protected row/policy before disclosure; boundary tracing required",p.name));continue;
                }
                if stability::implicit(bound,p).iter().any(|t|t==&inv.table||deps.contains(t)){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: implicit writes can change column policy before disclosure",p.name));continue;}
            }
            let args_list=histories::fixture_cases(&p.args,rows,&actors,out);
            for args in args_list{
                if out.column_observer_calls+4>16384{out.unsupported.insert("column observers capped at 16384 calls; remaining value worlds not covered".into());return;}
                out.column_observer_calls+=4;
                let execute=|alternate:bool|->Result<serde_json::Value,String>{
                    let seeds=if alternate{&other}else{rows};
                    let mut e=if let Some(fixture)=fixture{
                        let mut e=fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())?;
                        if alternate{
                            let t=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
                            let Value::Row(fields)=altrow else{return Err("not a row".into());};
                            let clean=Value::Row(fields.iter().filter(|(c,_)|!t.engine_field(c)&&!t.recorded.contains_key(*c)&&!t.maintained.contains_key(*c)).map(|(c,v)|(c.clone(),v.clone())).collect());
                            if let Err(why)=e.seed_at([(inv.table.clone(),clean.clone())],NOW){
                                let immutable=t.constraints.iter().any(|c|matches!(c,Constraint::Immutable{fields} if inv.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|fields.contains(c)))||matches!(c,Constraint::AppendOnly));
                                if !immutable{return Err(why.to_string());}
                                e=engine_on_store(bound,&other,Vec::new(),fixture.store().empty_with_asset_ledger().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?)?;
                            }
                        }e
                    }else{engine(bound,seeds,program("$column.pair",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]))?};
                    let actual=readback(&e)?;
                    let current=actual.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()).ok_or("column target is absent after validation")?;
                    if policy_in(bound,inv,&actual,viewer,None,current,&mut e)!=Ok(false){return Err("validated column-world policy is not false".into());}
                    if alternate&&fixture.is_some(){
                        for (table,rs) in rows.iter().filter(|(table,_)|bound.schema.maintain_for(table).is_none()){
                            let definition=&bound.schema.tables[table];
                            let same=actual.get(table).map_or(0,Vec::len)==rs.len()&&rs.iter().all(|old|actual.get(table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok()).is_some_and(|new|{
                                let (Value::Row(old_fields),Value::Row(new_fields))=(old,new)else{return false;};
                                old_fields.keys().chain(new_fields.keys()).filter(|c|!definition.maintained.contains_key(*c)&&!(table==&inv.table&&inv.column.as_ref()==Some(*c)&&old.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok())).all(|c|old.field(c).ok()==new.field(c).ok())
                            }));
                            if !same{return Err(format!("alternate seed changes non-protected base fields in {table}; capture/recorded reduction unavailable"));}
                        }
                    }
                    let actual_args=materialize_arguments(&mut e,&p.args,&args,viewer)?;
                    paired::observe(&mut e,p,viewer,actual_args)
                };
                let (a,b)=match (execute(false),execute(true)){
                    (Ok(a),Ok(b))=>(a,b),
                    (Err(why),_)|(_,Err(why))=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{} / {}: declined column world: {why}",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap_or(""),p.name));continue;}
                };
                out.attempted+=2;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=2;
                if a==b{continue;}
                if !matches!((execute(false),execute(true)),(Ok(aa),Ok(bb)) if aa==a&&bb==b){continue;}
                out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:if fixture.is_some(){Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace}else{Reachability::SyntheticOperation},invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:args.clone(),viewer:viewer.clone(),operation:p.name.clone(),effect:"column read observation".into(),row:row.clone(),old:Some(altrow.clone()),supporting_rows:trace.map(|(initial,_)|initial.clone()).unwrap_or_else(||rows.clone()),history:trace.map(|(_,steps)|steps.to_vec()).unwrap_or_default(),rule_path:format!("authored observer depends on {}.{} while policy is false in both typed worlds",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap()),scope:if fixture.is_some(){"primary observation executes on trusted fixture; alternate column is a dependency test, not an authored alternate-history claim"}else{"schema-valid synthetic worlds; authored observer, no application reachability claim"}.into(),observations:Some(paired::Observations{present:a,absent:b,absent_rows:other.clone()})});
                continue 'actors;
            }
        }
    }
}
fn run_pair(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,row:&Value,fixture:Option<&Engine>,trace:Option<(&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,&[histories::Step])>,out:&mut Campaign){
    run_pair_variant(bound,inv,rows,row,fixture,trace,false,out);
    if inv.column.as_ref().and_then(|c|bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].columns.get(c)).is_some_and(C::is_optional){run_pair_variant(bound,inv,rows,row,fixture,trace,true,out);}
}
pub(super) fn generated(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>){
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id")&&sites.is_none_or(|s|s.contains(&i.site.to_string()))){
        if inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(super::super::temporal::has_clock){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: column clock pairs use the campaign clock only; other temporal ticks not covered",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap()));}
        for variant in 0..16{
            let rows=match snapshot(bound,inv,variant){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column snapshot: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            let e=match engine(bound,&rows,program("$column.seed",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column seed: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            let Ok(rows)=readback(&e)else{continue;};
            for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){run_pair(bound,inv,&rows,row,None,None,out);}
            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()){break;}
        }
    }
}
pub(super) fn fixture(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    let Ok(rows)=readback(fixture)else{return;};
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id")){
        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){run_pair(bound,inv,&rows,row,Some(fixture),None,out);}
    }
}

pub(super) fn after_history(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,steps:&[histories::Step],out:&mut Campaign){
    let rows=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(why);return;}};
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id")){

        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){run_pair(bound,inv,&rows,row,Some(fixture),Some((initial,steps)),out);}
    }
}

The code keeps direct-rule theorems separate from observer obligations and finite witness campaigns. I’m now checking whether reported witnesses preserve authorized intent, valid paired worlds, write stability, and their claimed fixture reachability.

//! Real operation histories from a complete trusted fixture, including assets.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — only actual fixture traces establish application reachability.
use super::*;
const LIMIT:usize=512;
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize)]
pub struct Step {pub operation:String,pub viewer:String,pub args:BTreeMap<String,Value>,pub result:serde_json::Value}

pub(super) fn viewers(bound:&Bound)->Vec<String>{
    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([VIEWER.into(),OTHER.into(),"account:security-no-role".into(),"service:security".into()]);
    out.extend(bound.personas.iter().map(|p|if p.contains(':'){p.clone()}else{format!("dev:{p}")}));
    fn seats(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match value{
        serde_json::Value::String(s) if s.starts_with("service:")&&s.len()>8=>{out.insert(s.clone());},
        serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for x in xs{seats(x,out)},
        serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for x in xs.values(){seats(x,out)},_=>{}
    }}
    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.schema).unwrap(),&mut out);
    for p in &bound.programs{seats(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),&mut out);}
    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.invariants).unwrap(),&mut out);
    out.into_iter().collect()
}
fn actors(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>)->Vec<String>{
    let mut out=viewers(bound);
    for (table,rs) in rows{for row in rs{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{
        if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=row.field(c)&&!out.contains(p){out.push(p.clone());}
    }}}
    out
}
pub(super) fn fixture_cases(args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],out:&mut Campaign)->Vec<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    fn values(name:&str,k:&ir::ArgType,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String])->Option<Vec<Value>>{
        Some(match k{
            ir::ArgType::Enum{variants}=>variants.iter().map(|s|Value::String(s.clone())).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Bool=>vec![Value::Bool(false),Value::Bool(true)],
            ir::ArgType::Principal=>actors.iter().map(|s|Value::Id(s.clone())).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Ref{table} if rows.get(table).is_some_and(|rs|!rs.is_empty())=>rows[table].iter().filter_map(|r|r.field("id").ok().cloned()).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Optional(inner)=>{let mut v=vec![Value::Null];v.extend(values(name,inner,rows,actors)?);v},
            _=>vec![arguments(&BTreeMap::from([(name.into(),k.clone())]),0)?.remove(name)?],
        })
    }
    let mut cases=vec![BTreeMap::new()];
    for (name,k) in args{
        let Some(vals)=values(name,k,rows,actors)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{name}: history needs a typed asset argument"));return Vec::new();};
        let mut next=Vec::new();
        for n in 0..vals.len() {for c in &cases{
            let mut case=c.clone();case.insert(name.clone(),vals[n%vals.len()].clone());
            if !next.contains(&case){next.push(case);}if next.len()==LIMIT{break;}
        }if next.len()==LIMIT{out.unsupported.insert("history arguments capped at 512 combinations".into());break;}}
        cases=next;
    }cases
}
fn violation(out:&mut Campaign,inv:&Invariant,steps:&[Step],viewer:&str,row:&Value,old:Option<&Value>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,effect:&str){
    let committed=|steps:&[Step]|steps.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count();
    if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&committed(&v.history)==committed(steps)){return;}
    out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace,observations:None,history:steps.to_vec(),invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:BTreeMap::new(),viewer:viewer.into(),operation:steps.last().map(|s|s.operation.clone()).unwrap_or("$fixture.read".into()),effect:effect.into(),row:row.clone(),old:old.cloned(),supporting_rows:initial.clone(),rule_path:"actual authored operation/read from the trusted fixture".into(),scope:"reachable from recorded trusted fixture; finite trace, not an all-history proof".into()});
}
fn typed_disclosure(ty:&crate::types::Ty,data:&Value,table:&str,key:&Value,column:Option<&str>,physical:&Value)->bool{
    use crate::types::Ty;
    let payload=|data:&Value|matches!(data,Value::Row(_))&&data.field("id").ok()==Some(key)&&column.is_none_or(|c|data.field(c).ok().is_some_and(|v|physical.field(c).ok()==Some(v)));
    match ty{
        Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t) if t==table=>payload(data),
        Ty::Optional(inner)=>typed_disclosure(inner,data,table,key,column,physical),
        Ty::Page(t,_) if t==table=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(payload)),
        Ty::List(inner,_)|Ty::Collection(inner,_)=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(|r|typed_disclosure(inner,r,table,key,column,physical))),
        Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>fields.iter().any(|(name,ty)|data.field(name).ok().is_some_and(|v|typed_disclosure(ty,v,table,key,column,physical))),
        _=>false,
    }
}
fn typed_source(ty:&crate::types::Ty,table:&str)->bool{
    use crate::types::Ty;
    match ty{Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t)|Ty::Page(t,_)=>t==table,Ty::Optional(inner)|Ty::List(inner,_)|Ty::Collection(inner,_)=>typed_source(inner,table),Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>fields.values().any(|ty|typed_source(ty,table)),_=>false}
}
fn observer_tables(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->BTreeSet<String>{
    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([table.into()]);
    loop{let before=out.len();
        for m in &bound.schema.maintains{if out.contains(&m.source){out.insert(m.target.clone());if let Some(owner)=&m.private{out.insert(owner.table.clone());}}}
        for (name,t) in &bound.schema.tables{if t.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|edge|out.contains(&edge.parent))||[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).iter().any(|t|out.contains(t))){out.insert(name.clone());}}
        if out.len()==before{return out;}
    }
}
fn reserve_observers(out:&mut Campaign,calls:usize)->bool{
    if out.history_observer_calls+calls>32768{out.unsupported.insert("history read observers capped at 32768 calls; remaining disclosure combinations not covered".into());false}else{out.history_observer_calls+=calls;true}
}
fn observe(bound:&Bound,e:&mut Engine,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],steps:&[Step],out:&mut Campaign){
    if !reserve_observers(out,0)||out.history_observer_calls>=32768{return;}
    let queries=bound.programs.iter().filter(|bp|bp.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query&&!bp.program.native).filter(|bp|{
        let json=serde_json::to_string(&bp.program).unwrap();
        if ["NewId","Emit","JobAs","JobQuery"].iter().any(|tag|json.contains(tag)){
            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated-id/opaque history observer needs disclosure provenance",bp.program.name));false
        }else{true}
    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"){
        let direct=inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::implies(&super::super::columns::admission(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],inv.column.as_deref()),p)==Ok(true));
        let indirect=bound.schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.source==inv.table)||bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(&inv.table)));
        if direct&&!indirect{continue;}

        let observing=observer_tables(bound,&inv.table);
        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){'actors:for viewer in actors{
            let committed=steps.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count();
            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==*viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&v.history.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count()==committed){continue;}
            match policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,None,row,e){Ok(false)=>{},Ok(true)=>continue,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {why}",inv.target_label()));continue;}}
            let Ok(key)=row.field("id")else{continue;};
            if inv.column.as_ref().and_then(|c|bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].written_by.get(c)).is_some_and(|gate|!gate.audience&&row.field(&gate.owner).ok()!=Some(&Value::Id(viewer.clone()))){continue 'actors;}

            // A typed authored row return is already a payload witness. It does
            // not require an invalid absent world that deletes referenced data.
            for bp in queries.iter().copied().filter(|p|typed_source(&p.result,&inv.table)){
                let cases=fixture_cases(&bp.program.args,rows,actors,out);
                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed read-observer arguments capped at 32",bp.program.name));}
                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
                    if !reserve_observers(out,1){return;}out.attempted+=1;
                    if let Ok(q)=e.query(&bp.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW))&&q.complete&&!q.unavailable&&typed_disclosure(&bp.result,&q.data,&inv.table,key,inv.column.as_deref(),row){
                        violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,if inv.column.is_some(){"column read observation"}else{"read"});
                        if let Some(v)=out.violations.last_mut(){v.operation=bp.program.name.clone();v.args=args;v.rule_path="authored query returned a compiler-typed row/column payload of the protected table while current policy is false".into();}
                        continue 'actors;
                    }
                }
            }
            if inv.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"){continue 'actors;}
            if bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|t.columns.values().any(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Ref{table} if table==&inv.table))){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history paired absence needs a referentially valid closure",inv.table));continue;}
            let Ok(id)=key.as_str()else{continue;};
            let mut absent=match fork(bound,e,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world fork: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            if let Err(why)=absent.seed_delete_at(&inv.table,id,NOW){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world refused: {why}",inv.table));continue;}
            let absent_rows=match readback(&absent){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world readback: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            if policy_in(bound,inv,&absent_rows,viewer,None,row,&mut absent)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            for bp in queries.iter().copied().filter(|p|observing.iter().any(|t|super::super::audit::mentions(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),t))){
                let p=&bp.program;
                let cases=fixture_cases(&p.args,rows,actors,out);
                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: read-observer arguments capped at 32; remaining combinations not covered",p.name));}
                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
                    if !reserve_observers(out,4){return;}out.attempted+=2;
                    let (Ok(a),Ok(b))=(paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone()))else{continue;};
                    if a==b{continue;}
                    if !matches!((paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone())),(Ok(aa),Ok(bb)) if a==aa&&b==bb){continue;}
                    violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,"read");
                    if let Some(v)=out.violations.last_mut(){v.operation=p.name.clone();v.args=args;v.observations=Some(paired::Observations{present:a,absent:b,absent_rows:absent_rows.clone()});}
                    continue 'actors;
                }
            }
        }}
    }
}
fn prior_policy(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value,prior:&mut Option<Engine>)->Result<bool,String>{
    match prior.as_mut(){Some(e)=>policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row,e),None=>policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row)}
}
fn assertions(bound:&Bound,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="always"){
        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
            let old=before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok());
            if policy_in(bound,inv,after,viewer,old,row,current)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            // A pre-existing seed defect is supplemental; a newly violated
            // assertion or changed image is an authored committed observation.
            if old!=Some(row)||old.is_some_and(|r|prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(r),r,prior)==Ok(true)){
                violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,"always");
            }
        }
    }
}
fn changed(bound:&Bound,p:&Program,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    assertions(bound,before,after,initial,viewer,steps,prior,current,out);
    if !super::super::census::body(&p.body){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: loop/opaque write boundary requires executed-effect tracing",p.name));return;}
    for inv in &bound.invariants{
        if matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"read"|"always")||inv.operation.as_ref().is_some_and(|op|op!=&p.name){continue;}
        let judged;
        let inv=if inv.target=="always"{inv}else{
            let Some(stable)=stability::necessary(bound,p,inv)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}: unknown/implicit policy changes need executed-effect tracing",p.name,inv.target_label()));continue;};
            judged=stable;&judged
        };
        // A net image cannot identify an intermediate write or implicit effect.
        let writes=ir::write_tables(p).into_iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if writes.len()!=1 {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}.{}: multiple or implicit writes require executed-effect tracing",p.name,inv.table,inv.target));continue;}
        if (inv.target=="delete"||inv.operation.is_some())&&writes[0].1!="delete"&&before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|old|!after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok())){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: commit-end/implicit retirement is not an executed delete",p.name));}
        if (inv.target=="delete"||inv.operation.is_some())&&writes[0].1=="delete"{
            for old in before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|old|live(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],old)&&!after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok())){
                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
                if ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,_)|deps.contains(t)&&t!=&inv.table){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: delete policy dependency changes before disclosure",p.name));continue;}
                if prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(old),old,prior)==Ok(false){violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,old,Some(old),initial,"delete");}
            }
        }
        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
            let old=before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()&&live(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],r));
            if old==Some(row){continue;}
            let effect=if old.is_some(){"update"}else{"insert"};
            if !super::super::effects(inv).contains(&effect){continue;}
            if inv.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|old.is_some_and(|o|o.field(c).ok()==row.field(c).ok())){continue;}
            if inv.target=="always" {
                if policy_in(bound,inv,after,viewer,old,row,current)==Ok(false){violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,effect);}
            }else{
                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
                if ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,_)|deps.contains(t)&&t!=&inv.table) {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: write policy dependency changes within the operation; tracing required",p.name));continue;}
                if prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,old,row,prior)==Ok(false)||old.is_some_and(|old|prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(old),old,prior)==Ok(false)){
                    violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,effect);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
fn role_candidates<'a>(bound:&Bound,p:&'a Program,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],cases:&[BTreeMap<String,Value>],fixture:&Engine)->Vec<(&'a Program,String,BTreeMap<String,Value>)>{
    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut groups=Vec::new();
    let json=serde_json::to_string(p).unwrap();
    let Ok(mut judge)=fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())else{return out;};
    for (table,_) in ir::write_tables(p){
        let columns=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,&table);
        if !columns.iter().any(|c|json.contains(&format!("\"{c}\":"))){continue;}
        let t=&bound.schema.tables[&table];
        for row in initial.get(&table).into_iter().flatten(){
            let principal=t.columns.iter().find_map(|(c,k)|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal).then(||row.field(c).ok()).flatten());
            let Some(principal)=principal else{continue;};
            let mut targets=Vec::new();
            for case in cases{
                let mut args=case.clone();
                for (name,kind) in &p.args{match kind{
                    ir::ArgType::Principal=>{args.insert(name.clone(),principal.clone());},
                    ir::ArgType::Ref{table:target} if target==&table=>{if let Ok(id)=row.field("id"){args.insert(name.clone(),id.clone());}},_=>{}
                }}
                if !targets.contains(&args){targets.push(args);}
            }
            let mut row_cases=Vec::new();
            for actor in actors{
                let owner=columns.iter().filter_map(|c|t.written_by.get(c)).any(|a|row.field(&a.owner).ok()==Some(&Value::Id(actor.clone())));
                let gate=judge.fixture_policy(&t.update_rule_judged(),actor,Some(row),row,NOW).ok();
                if !owner&&gate!=Some(rules::Verdict::Readable){continue;}
                for args in &targets{row_cases.push((p,actor.clone(),args.clone()));}
            }
            groups.push(row_cases);
        }
    }
    for n in 0..groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for g in &groups{if let Some(case)=g.get(n){out.push(case.clone());}}}
    out
}
pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    if bound.invariants.is_empty(){return;}
    let initial=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
    if initial.values().all(Vec::is_empty){out.unsupported.insert("history needs a trusted fixture; generated snapshots have separate reachability labels".into());return;}
    if initial.values().map(Vec::len).sum::<usize>()>1000{out.unsupported.insert("history fixture exceeds 1000 rows".into());return;}
    let actors=actors(bound,&initial);
    let probes=Vec::new();
    let mut groups=Vec::new();
    for bp in bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!p.program.native){
        let cases=fixture_cases(&bp.program.args,&initial,&actors,out);
        let mut group=role_candidates(bound,&bp.program,&initial,&actors,&cases,fixture);
        // Diagonal order gives both actors and argument values an early turn.
        for shift in 0..actors.len(){for n in 0..cases.len().max(actors.len()){
            if cases.is_empty(){break;}
            group.push((&bp.program,actors[(n+shift)%actors.len()].clone(),cases[n%cases.len()].clone()));
        }}
        if !group.is_empty(){groups.push(group);}
    }
    let mut candidates=Vec::new();
    for n in 0..groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for g in &groups{if let Some(c)=g.get(n){candidates.push(c.clone());}}}
    let mut base=match fork(bound,fixture,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="always"){
        for row in initial.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){for viewer in &actors{
            if policy_in(bound,inv,&initial,viewer,None,row,&mut base)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            let mut witness=Campaign::default();violation(&mut witness,inv,&[],viewer,row,None,&initial,"always");
            if let Some(mut v)=witness.violations.pop(){v.reachability=Reachability::TrustedSeedOnly;v.operation="$fixture.assertion".into();v.rule_path="initial trusted fixture assertion; no authored mutation".into();v.scope="definite violation in trusted seed; no authored transition claimed; non-failing supplemental evidence".into();out.violations.push(v);}break;
        }}
    }
    observe(bound,&mut base,&initial,&initial,&actors,&[],out);
    // Breadth first, then diagonal pairs: no first step monopolizes the cap.
    let singles=candidates.len().min(LIMIT);
    for n in 0..singles+LIMIT {
        let indices=if n<singles{vec![n]}else if candidates.is_empty(){break;}else{let k=n-singles;vec![k%candidates.len(),(k/candidates.len()+k+1)%candidates.len()]};
        let mut engine=match fork(bound,fixture,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
        out.sequences+=1;let mut steps=Vec::new();
        for i in indices{
            let (p,viewer,args)=&candidates[i];let before=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
            let args=match materialize_arguments(&mut engine,&p.args,args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history asset arguments: {why}",p.name));break;}};
            let mut prior=if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.target!="read"&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(RuleExpr::has_reference)){match fork(bound,&engine,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>Some(e),Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(why);None}}}else{None};
            out.attempted+=1;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
            match engine.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){
                Ok(result)=>{
                    let sent=matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..});
                    steps.push(Step{operation:p.name.clone(),viewer:viewer.clone(),args:args.clone(),result:serde_json::to_value(&result.state).unwrap()});
                    let after=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
                    if sent{changed(bound,p,&before,&after,&initial,viewer,&steps,&mut prior,&mut engine,out);}else{out.refused+=1;}
                    if sent&&before!=after{observe(bound,&mut engine,&after,&initial,&actors,&steps,out);column_reads::after_history(bound,&engine,&initial,&steps,out);}
                },Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history engine error {e}",p.name));break;}
            }
        }
    }
    out.unsupported.insert("history breadth/pairs bounded to 512; unvisited and longer traces remain uncovered".into());
}

//! Conservative transitive write closure for pre-write policy snapshots.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#127-stable-judgments-and-direct-payload-witnesses — implicit writes cannot establish pre-write intent.
use super::*;
pub(super) fn implicit(bound:&Bound,p:&Program)->BTreeSet<String>{
    let mut affected=ir::write_tables(p).into_iter().map(|(t,_)|t).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    let mut implicit=BTreeSet::new();
    loop{
        let before=affected.len();
        for m in &bound.schema.maintains{if affected.contains(&m.source)||m.row_key.as_ref().is_some_and(|r|affected.contains(&r.table)){
            if bound.schema.table(&m.target).is_some(){implicit.insert(m.target.clone());affected.insert(m.target.clone());}
            if let Some(owner)=&m.private{implicit.insert(owner.table.clone());affected.insert(owner.table.clone());}
        }}
        for (name,t) in &bound.schema.tables{
            if t.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|edge|edge.live.is_some()&&affected.contains(&edge.parent)){implicit.insert(name.clone());affected.insert(name.clone());}
            for (column,action) in &t.on_delete{
            if action.writes()&&let Some(C::Ref{table})=t.columns.get(column).map(C::required)&&affected.contains(table){implicit.insert(name.clone());affected.insert(name.clone());}
        }}
        if before==affected.len(){break;}
    }implicit
}

pub(super) fn dependencies(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr)->BTreeSet<String>{
    let mut deps=kernel::dependencies(rule);
    loop{let before=deps.len();for name in deps.clone(){if let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(&name){deps.extend(kernel::dependencies(&t.rules.read));if let Some(edge)=&t.through{deps.insert(edge.parent.clone());}}}if before==deps.len(){return deps;}}
}
/// A stable necessary condition may refute an intent even when another
/// conjunct changes. Dropping a requirement never proves the full policy.
pub(super) fn necessary(bound:&Bound,p:&Program,inv:&Invariant)->Option<Invariant>{
    let policy=inv.policy.as_ref()?;let implicit=implicit(bound,p);
    if implicit.contains(&inv.table){return None;}
    let mut changed=implicit;
    changed.extend(ir::write_tables(p).into_iter().filter(|(t,_)|t!=&inv.table).map(|(t,_)|t));
    fn weak(bound:&Bound,r:&RuleExpr,changed:&BTreeSet<String>)->RuleExpr{
        match r{
            RuleExpr::And(xs)=>RuleExpr::And(xs.iter().map(|r|weak(bound,r,changed)).collect()),
            RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>RuleExpr::Or(xs.iter().map(|r|weak(bound,r,changed)).collect()),
            _ if dependencies(bound,r).iter().any(|d|changed.contains(d))=>RuleExpr::Allow,
            _=>r.clone(),
        }
    }
    let weaker=weak(bound,policy,&changed);
    if kernel::implies(policy,&weaker)!=Ok(true){return None;}
    let mut judged=inv.clone();judged.policy=Some(weaker);Some(judged)
}
//! Expand advisory write sites with lexical arguments and dominating guards.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#128-runtime-intent-and-independent-worlds — helper placement cannot hide a grant writer.
use super::*;
use ast::{Expr,Stmt,ProgramDecl,FunctionBody};
type Env=BTreeMap<String,Expr>;
fn resolve(expr:&Expr,env:&Env)->Expr{
    let mut expr=expr.clone();
    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut expr,&mut |e|{
        if let Expr::Object{fields,..}|Expr::Write{fields,..}=e{for f in fields{if f.value.is_none(){f.value=Some(env.get(&f.name).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||Expr::Name{name:f.name.clone(),site:f.site.clone()}));}}}
        if let Expr::Name{name,..}=e&&let Some(value)=env.get(name){*e=value.clone();}
    });expr
}
fn expression(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl,expr:&Expr,env:&Env,guards:&[Stmt],depth:usize,out:&mut Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>){
    let mut expr=resolve(expr,env);
    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut expr,&mut |e|match e{
        Expr::Write{..}=>{let mut context=p.clone();context.prelude=Vec::new();context.body=guards.to_vec();out.push((e.clone(),context));},
        Expr::Call{name,args,..} if depth<32=>if let Some(f)=catalog.functions.get(name){
            let mut locals=f.args.iter().zip(args).map(|(arg,value)|(arg.name.clone(),value.clone())).collect();
            let mut inherited=guards.to_vec();
            match &f.body{
                FunctionBody::Expression(value)=>expression(catalog,p,value,&locals,&inherited,depth+1,out),
                FunctionBody::Block(body)=>statements(catalog,p,body,&mut locals,&mut inherited,depth+1,out),
            }
        },_=>{}
    });
}
// Only an unconditional direct call exports a helper's guaranteed prefix.
// Conditional/loop-local requirements never become caller assumptions.
fn helper_guards(catalog:&Catalog,expr:&Expr,env:&Env,depth:usize)->Vec<Stmt>{
    if depth>=32{return Vec::new();}
    let Expr::Call{name,args,..}=resolve(expr,env)else{return Vec::new();};
    let Some(f)=catalog.functions.get(&name)else{return Vec::new();};
    let mut locals=f.args.iter().zip(args).map(|(a,v)|(a.name.clone(),v)).collect::<Env>();
    let FunctionBody::Block(body)=&f.body else{return if let FunctionBody::Expression(value)=&f.body{helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1)}else{Vec::new()};};
    let mut out=Vec::new();
    for s in body{match s{
        Stmt::Require{condition,code,message,site}=>out.push(Stmt::Require{condition:resolve(condition,&locals),code:code.clone(),message:message.clone(),site:site.clone()}),
        Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=>{out.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1));if names.len()==1{locals.insert(names[0].clone(),resolve(value,&locals));}},
        Stmt::Expr{value,..}=>out.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1)),
        Stmt::Return{value,..}=>{out.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1));break;},_=>break,
    }}out
}
fn statements(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl,body:&[Stmt],env:&mut Env,guards:&mut Vec<Stmt>,depth:usize,out:&mut Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>){
    for s in body{match s{
        Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,depth,out);guards.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,env,0));if names.len()==1{env.insert(names[0].clone(),resolve(value,env));}},
        Stmt::Require{condition,code,message,site}=>{
            expression(catalog,p,condition,env,guards,depth,out);
            guards.push(Stmt::Require{condition:resolve(condition,env),code:code.clone(),message:message.clone(),site:site.clone()});
        },
        Stmt::Return{value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,depth,out);break;},
        Stmt::Expr{value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,depth,out);guards.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,env,0));},
        Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body,..}=>{
            expression(catalog,p,condition,env,guards,depth,out);
            statements(catalog,p,then_body,&mut env.clone(),&mut guards.clone(),depth,out);
            statements(catalog,p,else_body,&mut env.clone(),&mut guards.clone(),depth,out);
        },
        Stmt::For{domain,name,body,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,domain,env,guards,depth,out);let mut local=env.clone();local.remove(name);statements(catalog,p,body,&mut local,&mut guards.clone(),depth,out);},
        Stmt::As{body,..}=>statements(catalog,p,body,&mut env.clone(),&mut guards.clone(),depth,out),_=>{}
    }}
}
pub(super) fn writes(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl)->Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>{
    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut env=Env::new();let mut guards=Vec::new();
    statements(catalog,p,&p.prelude,&mut env,&mut guards,0,&mut out);
    statements(catalog,p,&p.body,&mut env,&mut guards,0,&mut out);out
}
//! Role authority is compared by effective gates, never by names like 'admin'.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#123-actionable-authority-advice — advisory grant-edge analysis, not a security proof or implicit exemption.
use super::*;
pub(super) fn probe_pairs(bound:&Bound,table:&str,index:&str,key:&[RuleTerm],equalities:&[(String,RuleTerm)])->Vec<(String,RuleTerm)>{
    let mut pairs=equalities.to_vec();
    if let Some(idx)=bound.schema.table(table).and_then(|t|t.index(index)){for (component,term) in idx.components.iter().zip(key){if let Some(column)=component.column(){pairs.push((column.into(),term.clone()));}}}
    pairs
}
pub(super) fn global_principal(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->Option<String>{
    let t=bound.schema.table(table)?;
    t.indexes.values().find_map(|index|{
        if !index.unique||index.components.len()!=1{return None;}
        let column=index.components[0].column()?;
        matches!(t.columns.get(column),Some(snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal)).then(||column.to_string())
    })
}
pub(super) fn membership_probe(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,roster:&str)->bool{
    let RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..}=rule else{return false;};
    if table!=roster{return false;}
    let Some(principal)=global_principal(bound,table)else{return false;};
    let pairs=probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities);
    pairs.len()==1&&pairs[0].0==principal&&pairs[0].1==RuleTerm::Viewer
}
pub(super) fn membership_grant_risk(bound:&Bound,roster:&str,operation:&str,requirements:&[RuleExpr],op:ast::WriteOp)->bool{
    if op==ast::WriteOp::Upsert{return membership_grant_risk(bound,roster,operation,requirements,ast::WriteOp::Insert)||membership_grant_risk(bound,roster,operation,requirements,ast::WriteOp::Update);}
    fn honors(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,roster:&str)->bool{match rule{
        RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|r|honors(bound,r,roster)),
        r=>membership_probe(bound,r,roster),
    }}
    if global_principal(bound,roster).is_none()||!bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|honors(bound,r,roster))){return false;}
    let table=&bound.schema.tables[roster];
    let mut branches=match op {ast::WriteOp::Insert=>table.rules.insert.disjuncts(),ast::WriteOp::Update=>table.rules.update.disjuncts(),_=>return false}.into_iter().filter(|r|r.service_grant().is_none()).cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if op==ast::WriteOp::Update&&let Some(owner)=global_principal(bound,roster).and_then(|c|table.written_by.get(&c)){branches.push(RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(owner.owner.clone()),RuleTerm::Viewer));}
    let admission=RuleExpr::And(std::iter::once(RuleExpr::Or(branches)).chain(requirements.iter().cloned()).collect());
    let ordinary=substitute(bound,&admission,"$no-roster","$membership",None,None);
    bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.table==roster&&i.column.is_none()&&(i.operation.as_deref()==Some(operation)||i.operation.is_none()&&(i.target=="write"||i.target==if op==ast::WriteOp::Insert{"insert"}else{"update"}))).filter_map(|i|i.policy.as_ref()).any(|p|super::operation::safe(p)&&kernel::implies(&ordinary,p)==Ok(false))
}
fn substitute(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,roster:&str,column:&str,role:Option<&str>,target:Option<&str>)->RuleExpr{
    let known=|t:&RuleTerm|match t{RuleTerm::Field(c) if c==column=>target.map(|s|snapback4_core::Value::String(s.into())),RuleTerm::Literal(v)=>Some(v.clone()),_=>None};
    match rule{
        RuleExpr::Exists{table,key,index,equalities,..} if table==roster&&key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)|t)).any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer))=>{
            let pairs=probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities);
            let requested=pairs.iter().find(|(c,_)|c==column).and_then(|(_,t)|if let RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(s))=t{Some(s.as_str())}else{None});
            if requested.is_none(){rule.clone()}else if requested==role{
                if pairs.iter().all(|(c,t)|c==column||matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer)){RuleExpr::Allow}else{rule.clone()}
            }else{RuleExpr::Deny}
        },
        RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Id(seat)))|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Id(seat)),RuleTerm::Viewer) if seat.starts_with("service:")=>RuleExpr::Deny,
        RuleExpr::Eq(a,b)|RuleExpr::Ne(a,b)=>if let (Some(a),Some(b))=(known(a),known(b)){if (a==b)==matches!(rule,RuleExpr::Eq(..)){RuleExpr::Allow}else{RuleExpr::Deny}}else{rule.clone()},
        RuleExpr::And(xs)=>RuleExpr::And(xs.iter().map(|r|substitute(bound,r,roster,column,role,target)).collect()),
        RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>RuleExpr::Or(xs.iter().map(|r|substitute(bound,r,roster,column,role,target)).collect()),
        RuleExpr::Not(r)=>RuleExpr::Not(Box::new(substitute(bound,r,roster,column,role,target))),
        _=>rule.clone(),
    }
}
pub(super) fn risk(bound:&Bound,roster:&str,column:&str,operation:&str,targets:&[String],op:ast::WriteOp,requirements:&[RuleExpr],dynamic:bool)->Option<String>{
    if op==ast::WriteOp::Upsert{return risk(bound,roster,column,operation,targets,ast::WriteOp::Insert,requirements,dynamic).or_else(||risk(bound,roster,column,operation,targets,ast::WriteOp::Update,requirements,dynamic));}
    let table=bound.schema.table(roster)?;
    if requirements.iter().any(|r|!r.disjuncts().is_empty()&&r.disjuncts().iter().all(|b|b.service_grant().is_some())){return None;}
    let gates=match op{ast::WriteOp::Insert=>vec![&table.rules.insert],ast::WriteOp::Update=>vec![&table.rules.update],ast::WriteOp::Upsert=>vec![&table.rules.insert,&table.rules.update],ast::WriteOp::Delete=>return None};
    let roles=match table.columns.get(column)?.required(){snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Enum{variants}=>variants.clone(),snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Text{..}=>{
        let mut roles=BTreeSet::new();
        fn collect(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,roster:&str,column:&str,roles:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match rule{
            RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..} if table==roster=>for (c,t) in &probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities){if c==column&&let RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(s))=t{roles.insert(s.clone());}},
            RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for r in xs{collect(bound,r,roster,column,roles)},RuleExpr::Not(r)=>collect(bound,r,roster,column,roles),_=>{}
        }}
        for t in bound.schema.tables.values(){for r in [&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete]{collect(bound,r,roster,column,&mut roles);}}roles.into_iter().collect()
    },_=>return None};
    let intents=bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.table==roster&&i.column.as_deref().is_none_or(|c|c==column)&&(i.operation.as_deref()==Some(operation)||i.operation.is_none()&&(i.target=="write"||i.target==if op==ast::WriteOp::Insert{"insert"}else{"update"}))).filter_map(|i|i.policy.as_ref()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    fn role_guards(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,out:&mut Vec<RuleExpr>){match rule{
        RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..} if key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,v)|v)).any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer))&&probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities).iter().any(|(c,v)|role_columns(bound,table).contains(c)&&matches!(v,RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(_))))=>out.push(rule.clone()),
        RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for r in xs{role_guards(bound,r,out)},_=>{}
    }}
    let mut all_branches=gates.iter().flat_map(|r|r.disjuncts()).filter(|b|b.service_grant().is_none()).cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if op!=ast::WriteOp::Insert&&let Some(owner)=table.written_by.get(column){all_branches.push(RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(owner.owner.clone()),RuleTerm::Viewer));}
    let admission=RuleExpr::And(std::iter::once(RuleExpr::Or(all_branches)).chain(requirements.iter().cloned()).collect());
    let mut guards=Vec::new();role_guards(bound,&admission,&mut guards);
    let independent=kernel::implies(&substitute(bound,&admission,"$no-roster",column,None,None),&RuleExpr::Or(guards))==Ok(true);
    for target in roles.iter().filter(|r|targets.is_empty()||targets.contains(r)){
        for caller in std::iter::once(None).chain(roles.iter().map(|s|Some(s.as_str()))){
            if caller==Some(target.as_str()){continue;}
            let admitted=substitute(bound,&admission,roster,column,caller,Some(target));
            let admits=kernel::implies(&admitted,&RuleExpr::Deny)!=Ok(true);
            if !admits{continue;}
            let violates_intent=intents.iter().any(|p|kernel::implies(&admitted,&substitute(bound,p,roster,column,caller,Some(target)))==Ok(false));
            // Existing independently gated appointers may delegate different
            // roles. Independent intent can restrict a particular target role.
            if independent&&!violates_intent{continue;}
            if violates_intent{return Some(format!("{roster}.{column} assignment to '{target}' is not established by the independent delegation intent for {}",caller.unwrap_or("a caller without a role")));}
            for (name,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){for (effect,rule) in [("read",&t.rules.read),("insert",&t.rules.insert),("update",&t.rules.update),("delete",&t.rules.delete)]{
                let granted=substitute(bound,rule,roster,column,Some(target),None);let previous=substitute(bound,rule,roster,column,caller,None);
                if kernel::implies(&RuleExpr::Allow,&granted)!=Ok(true){continue;}
                if !dynamic&&!(matches!(effect,"read"|"delete")||!role_columns(bound,name).is_empty()){continue;}
                if kernel::implies(&granted,&previous)==Ok(false){return Some(format!("{} can confer '{target}', which adds {name}.{effect} authority not held by {}",roster,caller.unwrap_or("a caller without a role")));}
            }}
        }
    }None
}

pub(super) fn role_columns(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->BTreeSet<String>{
    let mut columns=BTreeSet::new();
    if let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(table){for c in ["role","kind"]{if t.columns.contains_key(c){columns.insert(c.into());}}}
    fn collect(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,table:&str,columns:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match rule{
        RuleExpr::Exists{table:t,index,key,equalities,..} if t==table&&key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,v)|v)).any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer))=>for (c,v) in &probe_pairs(bound,t,index,key,equalities){if matches!(v,RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(_))){columns.insert(c.clone());}},
        RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for r in xs{collect(bound,r,table,columns)},RuleExpr::Not(r)=>collect(bound,r,table,columns),_=>{}
    }}
    let named=columns.clone();let mut observed=BTreeSet::new();
    for t in bound.schema.tables.values(){for r in [&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete]{collect(bound,r,table,&mut observed);}}
    let global=bound.schema.table(table).is_some_and(|t|t.indexes.values().any(|index|{
        if !index.unique{return false;}
        let Some(parts)=index.components.iter().map(|p|p.column()).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()else{return false;};
        let principals=parts.iter().filter(|c|matches!(t.columns.get(**c).map(|k|k.required()),Some(snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal))).count();
        if principals!=1{return false;}
        parts.iter().all(|c|matches!(t.columns.get(*c).map(|k|k.required()),Some(snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal))||named.contains(*c))
            ||named.is_empty()&&observed.len()==1&&parts.len()==2&&parts.iter().all(|c|observed.contains(*c)||matches!(t.columns.get(*c).map(|k|k.required()),Some(snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal)))
    }));
    if global{for t in bound.schema.tables.values(){for r in [&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete]{collect(bound,r,table,&mut columns);}}}else{columns.clear();}
    columns
}

//! Advice identifies a risk shape; it never supplies a proof assumption.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#123-actionable-authority-advice — current roles, grant writers, required evidence and refusal oracles.
use super::*;
use crate::ast::{Expr,Stmt,ProgramKind};
const NOW_FOR_DEFAULTS:i64=1_800_000_000_000;
fn rule_parts(rule:&RuleExpr)->Vec<&RuleExpr>{match rule{RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().flat_map(rule_parts).collect(),RuleExpr::Not(x)=>rule_parts(x),r=>vec![r]}}
fn role_test(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr)->bool {rule_parts(rule).iter().any(|r|match r{
    RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..} if key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,v)|v)).any(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer))=>{
        if super::privilege::membership_probe(bound,r,table){return true;}
        let columns=super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table);
        super::privilege::probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities).iter().any(|(c,t)|columns.contains(c)&&matches!(t,RuleTerm::Literal(_)))
    },_=>false,
})}
fn reader_columns(rule:&RuleExpr)->BTreeSet<String>{rule_parts(rule).into_iter().filter_map(|r|match r{RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(f),RuleTerm::Viewer)|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Field(f))=>Some(f.clone()),_=>None}).collect()}
fn note(notes:&mut Vec<Diagnostic>,site:&Site,code:&str,message:String,rewrite:&str){notes.push(Diagnostic::new(site,code,message,rewrite).guide("security"));}
fn mutable_role(bound:&Bound,name:&str)->bool {
    let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(name)else{return false;};
    let mut roles=super::privilege::role_columns(bound,name);
    if roles.is_empty()&&let Some(principal)=super::privilege::global_principal(bound,name){roles.insert(principal);}
    if roles.is_empty(){return false;}
    let mutable=roles.iter().any(|c|!t.constraints.iter().any(|k|matches!(k,snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::Immutable{fields} if fields.contains(c))));
    fn supplied(value:&serde_json::Value,roles:&BTreeSet<String>)->bool{
        if let Some(fields)=value.get("Object").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object){return fields.keys().any(|c|roles.contains(c));}
        if let Some(fields)=value.get("Literal").and_then(|v|v.get("Map").or_else(||v.get("Row"))).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object){return fields.keys().any(|c|roles.contains(c));}
        true // Dynamic patches cannot establish that role fields are untouched.
    }
    fn writes(value:&serde_json::Value,name:&str,roles:&BTreeSet<String>,update:bool,delete:bool)->bool{
        match value{
            serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>{
                for tag in ["Update","Upsert","WriteTarget"]{if let Some(body)=xs.get(tag){
                    let target=if tag=="WriteTarget"{body.get("target").and_then(|v|v.get("table"))}else{body.get("table")};
                    if update&&target.and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some(name)&&body.get(if tag=="Upsert"{"row"}else{"patch"}).is_some_and(|v|supplied(v,roles)){return true;}
                }}
                for tag in ["Delete","DeleteTarget"]{if let Some(body)=xs.get(tag){
                    let target=if tag=="DeleteTarget"{body.get("target").and_then(|v|v.get("table"))}else{body.get("table")};
                    if delete&&target.and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some(name){return true;}
                }}
                xs.values().any(|v|writes(v,name,roles,update,delete))
            },serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|v|writes(v,name,roles,update,delete)),_=>false,
        }
    }
    bound.programs.iter().any(|p|{
        let system=p.program.kind==snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Job;
        let update=mutable&&(system||!matches!(t.rules.update,RuleExpr::Deny)||roles.iter().any(|c|t.written_by.contains_key(c)));
        let delete=system||!matches!(t.rules.delete,RuleExpr::Deny);
        writes(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),name,&roles,update,delete)
    })
}
fn recipient_role(bound:&Bound,name:&str,branch:&RuleExpr,grant:Option<&str>,roster:&str,effect:&str)->bool{
    if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.table==*name&&(i.target==effect||i.target=="write"&&matches!(effect,"insert"|"update"))&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::dependencies(p).contains(roster)&&role_test(bound,p)&&kernel::implies(branch,p)!=Ok(true))){return true;}
    let _=(branch,grant,roster);
    // Field names cannot establish a current-role contract. Without independent
    // intent, retained access may be deliberate, so lifetime advice stays silent.
    false
}
pub(super) fn notes(bound:&Bound,exposures:&[Exposure])->Vec<Diagnostic>{
    let mut notes=Vec::new();
    for (name,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){
        if t.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable(){note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_BROAD_READ",format!("{name} is readable by every signed-in user: {}",t.rules.read.quarry()),"confirm the spec permits these columns and declare read intent; snapback4 audit lists inherited grants and their writers");}
        if let RuleExpr::Exists{table:roster,key,equalities,..}=&t.rules.read
            && equalities.is_empty()&&key.iter().any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer)) {
            note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_BROAD_READ",format!("{name} is readable by any signed-in user with a matching {roster} row; no particular role or row ownership is required"),"confirm that every roster member should see all these columns; declare intent from the privacy spec and inspect audit");
        }
        for (column,kind) in &t.columns {
            use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType as C;
            if kind.contains_asset() && (kind.is_optional() || matches!(kind.required(),C::List{..}|C::ListRange{min:0,..})) {
                note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_OPTIONAL_EVIDENCE",format!("{name}.{column} permits omitted or empty media evidence"),"if the spec requires evidence, declare a required image/file or [image] 1..N; otherwise retain optionality deliberately (guide security)");
            }
        }
        for leaf in rule_parts(&t.rules.insert) {
            if let RuleExpr::Created {table:parent,..}=leaf
                && bound.schema.table(parent).is_some_and(|p| p.rules.insert.disjuncts().iter().any(|r| matches!(r,RuleExpr::Allow|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(_),RuleTerm::Viewer)|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Field(_)))))
                && bound.schema.tables.values().any(|other| [&other.rules.read,&other.rules.insert,&other.rules.update,&other.rules.delete].iter().any(|r| rule_parts(r).iter().any(|r|matches!(r,RuleExpr::Exists{table,key,equalities,..} if table==name&&matches!(key.as_slice(),[RuleTerm::Viewer])&&equalities.is_empty())))) {
                note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_VACUOUS_RULE",format!("{name}: `created {parent}` can bootstrap authority trusted globally by a viewer-only probe; a caller may create their own global administrator grant"),"provision global authority only through an operator/service seat or existing authorized admin; group creation must scope every later grant to that group (guide deploy, first-admin setup)");
            }
        }
        for (effect,rule) in [("read",&t.rules.read),("insert",&t.rules.insert),("update",&t.rules.update),("delete",&t.rules.delete)] {
          for branch in rule.disjuncts(){
            for dep in kernel::dependencies(branch){if let Some(g)=bound.schema.table(&dep) {
                let rosters=[&g.rules.insert,&g.rules.update].into_iter().flat_map(kernel::dependencies).filter(|r|r!=&dep&&mutable_role(bound,r)).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
                if rosters.iter().any(|r|recipient_role(bound,name,branch,Some(&dep),r,effect))&&(role_test(bound,&g.rules.insert)||role_test(bound,&g.rules.update)){
                    note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE",format!("{name}.{effect}: `{}` trusts retained {dep} grants while current role in {} can change",branch.quarry(),rosters.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")),"recheck the current role in this branch or revoke every derived grant transactionally on role changes; guide security");
                }
            }}
            let declared=bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.table==*name&&(i.target==effect||i.target=="write"&&matches!(effect,"insert"|"update"))).filter_map(|i|i.policy.as_ref()).flat_map(kernel::dependencies).filter(|r|mutable_role(bound,r)).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
            if !reader_columns(branch).is_empty()&&declared.iter().any(|r|recipient_role(bound,name,branch,None,r,effect)) {
                note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE",format!("{name}.{effect}: principal branch `{}` does not recheck the declared mutable current role",branch.quarry()),"recheck the current role as well as principal attribution; guide security");
            }
          }
        }
    }
    // Inspect original syntax for argument flow and distinct authored codes.
    // Keeping lints outside Bound.notes preserves the compiler's existing
    // diagnostic contract; check/audit expose this security advice separately.
    let _=exposures;
    let mut retained=BTreeSet::new();
    notes.retain(|n|n.code!="N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE"||retained.insert(n.message.split(':').next().unwrap_or("").to_string()));
    notes
}
pub(super) fn source_notes(sources:&[(String,String)],bound:&Bound)->Vec<Diagnostic>{
    let mut out=Vec::new();
    let documents=sources.iter().map(|(file,source)|crate::parse_document(file,source).0).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let catalog=super::super::catalog::Catalog::build(documents.clone(),&mut Vec::new());
    for doc in documents{
        for p in &doc.programs {
            let mut body=p.prelude.clone();body.extend(p.body.clone());
            let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
            let mut guarded=BTreeSet::new();
            for statement in p.prelude.iter().chain(&p.body) {
                if let Stmt::Require{condition,..}=statement {
                    let mut condition=condition.clone();
                    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut condition,&mut |e|if let Expr::Exists{read,..}=e {
                        for key in &read.keys {if let Expr::Name{name,..}=&key.value {guarded.insert(name.clone());}}
                    });
                }
            }
            for (write,context) in super::authority_walk::writes(&catalog,p){
                let Expr::Write{op,table,fields,site,..}=&write else{continue;};
                let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(table)else{continue;};
                let role_columns=super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table);
                let principal=super::privilege::global_principal(bound,table);
                let reassigns=principal.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|fields.iter().any(|f|&f.name==c)&&!t.constraints.iter().any(|k|matches!(k,snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::Immutable{fields} if fields.contains(c))));
                if role_columns.is_empty()&&p.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&(matches!(op,ast::WriteOp::Insert|ast::WriteOp::Upsert)||*op==ast::WriteOp::Update&&reassigns){
                    let operation=super::super::operation_name(std::path::Path::new(&doc.file).file_stem().unwrap().to_string_lossy().as_ref(),&p.name);
                    let requirements=catalog.tables.get(table).map(|info|super::operation::requirements(&catalog,info,&context)).unwrap_or_default();
                    if super::privilege::membership_grant_risk(bound,table,&operation,&requirements,if *op==ast::WriteOp::Upsert&&!reassigns{ast::WriteOp::Insert}else{*op})&&seen.insert((table.clone(),"$membership".into())){
                        note(&mut out,site,"N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION",format!("{}: {table} membership grants authority, but assignment does not establish the independent provisioning intent",p.name),"restrict membership grants to the declared provisioner; use guide staging's first-admin recipe");
                    }
                }
                for column in &role_columns {if p.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&(fields.iter().any(|f|&f.name==column)||matches!(op,ast::WriteOp::Insert|ast::WriteOp::Upsert)&&t.defaults.contains_key(column))&&bound.schema.tables.values().any(|other|[&other.rules.read,&other.rules.insert,&other.rules.update,&other.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(table))) {
                    let mut targets=fields.iter().filter(|f|&f.name==column).filter_map(|f|match &f.value{Some(Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Str(s),..})=>Some(s.clone()),_=>None}).collect::<Vec<_>>();
                    if !fields.iter().any(|f|&f.name==column)&&let Some(snapback4_core::Value::String(role))=t.defaults.get(column).map(|d|d.value(NOW_FOR_DEFAULTS,"$advisory")){targets.push(role);}
                    let chosen=fields.iter().find(|f|&f.name==column);
                    let dynamic=chosen.map_or(targets.is_empty(),|f|!matches!(f.value,Some(Expr::Literal{..})));
                    if let Some(field)=chosen {
                        if let Some(Expr::Name{name:argument,..})=&field.value&&let Some(arg)=p.args.iter().find(|a|a.name==*argument)&&let ast::TypeKind::Enum{variants}= &arg.type_.kind {targets=variants.clone();}
                    }
                    let requirements=catalog.tables.get(table).map(|info|super::operation::requirements(&catalog,info,&context)).unwrap_or_default();
                    let effective_op=if chosen.is_none()&&*op==ast::WriteOp::Upsert{ast::WriteOp::Insert}else{*op};
                    if let Some(risk)=super::privilege::risk(bound,table,column,&super::super::operation_name(std::path::Path::new(&doc.file).file_stem().unwrap().to_string_lossy().as_ref(),&p.name),&targets,effective_op,&requirements,dynamic)&&seen.insert((table.clone(),format!("$privilege:{column}"))) {
                        note(&mut out,site,"N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION",format!("{}: {risk}; review the grant's delegation policy",p.name),"restrict role assignment to authorized delegation or an operator seat; use guide staging's first-admin recipe (intentional open enrollment may permit this)");
                    }
                }}
            }
            super::super::intent_walk::statements(&mut body,&mut |e|{
                let Expr::Write{table,fields,site,..}=e else{return};
                let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(table)else{return};
                let readers=reader_columns(&t.rules.read);
                for f in fields.iter(){
                    let arg=f.value.as_ref().and_then(|e|if let Expr::Name{name,..}=e{Some(name.as_str())}else{None}).unwrap_or(f.name.as_str());
                    if !guarded.contains(arg)&&readers.contains(&f.name)&&p.args.iter().any(|a|a.name==arg)&&seen.insert((table.clone(),f.name.clone())){
                        note(&mut out,site,"N_ARGUMENT_GRANT",format!("{} writes argument {arg} straight into reader-grant column {table}.{}",p.name,f.name),"require the chosen principal's current role and relationship before granting access; caller authority alone does not validate the recipient");
                    }

                }
            });
            // Distinct absent and denial codes disclose logical existence to
            // callers admitted by a broader table rule, even without payload.
            fn refusals(body:&[Stmt],bound:&Bound,absent:&mut Option<String>,codes:&mut Vec<(Site,String)>){for s in body{match s{
                Stmt::Bind{value:Expr::Assert{value,code,..},..}=>{
                    *absent=None;
                    if let Expr::Read(read)=value.as_ref()&&let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(&read.table){
                        let own=read.keys.iter().any(|k|matches!(&k.value,Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="viewer"));
                        if !t.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable()&&!own {
                            // A table read gate already conflates hidden and absent.
                            // Only broader program-level privacy intent can make
                            // its later refusal distinguish private existence.
                            if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.table==read.table&&i.target=="read"&&i.column.is_none()&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|policy|kernel::implies(&t.rules.read,policy)==Ok(false))){*absent=Some(code.clone());}
                        }
                    }
                },
                Stmt::Require{site,code,..}=>if let Some(a)=absent.as_ref()&&a!=code{codes.push((site.clone(),format!("lookup absence {a}, later denial {code}")));},
                Stmt::If{then_body,else_body,..}=>{refusals(then_body,bound,&mut absent.clone(),codes);refusals(else_body,bound,&mut absent.clone(),codes);},
                Stmt::For{body,..}|Stmt::As{body,..}=>refusals(body,bound,&mut absent.clone(),codes),_=>{}}}}
            let mut codes=Vec::new();refusals(&p.body,bound,&mut None,&mut codes);
            for (site,why) in codes.into_iter().take(1){note(&mut out,&site,"N_EXISTENCE_ORACLE",format!("{}: {why}; row readership is broader than declared private-existence intent",p.name),"use the same absence/denial code and message for private existence, or tighten the row audience");}
            if p.kind==ProgramKind::Job {
                fn guards(body:&[Stmt],out:&mut Vec<Expr>){for s in body {match s{
                    Stmt::Require{condition,..}=>out.push(condition.clone()),
                    Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body,..}=>{out.push(condition.clone());guards(then_body,out);guards(else_body,out);},
                    Stmt::For{body,..}|Stmt::As{body,..}=>guards(body,out),_=>{}
                }}}
                let mut job_guards=Vec::new();guards(&p.body,&mut job_guards);
                let clock=job_guards.iter().any(|g|{let mut g=g.clone();let mut found=false;super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut g,&mut |e|if matches!(e,Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="now"){found=true;});found});
                if !clock{continue;}
                let mut writes=BTreeSet::new();let mut b=p.body.clone();
                super::super::intent_walk::statements(&mut b,&mut |e|if let Expr::Write{table,fields,..}=e&&fields.iter().any(|f|f.name=="status"){writes.insert(table.clone());});
                for table in writes {for m in doc.programs.iter().filter(|m|m.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation){
                    let mut aliases=BTreeSet::new();
                    for s in &m.body{if let Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=s{
                        let v=if let Expr::Assert{value,..}=value{value.as_ref()}else{value};
                        if matches!(v,Expr::Read(r) if r.table==table){aliases.extend(names.clone());}
                    }}
                    let mut checks=Vec::new();guards(&m.body,&mut checks);let(mut status,mut now)=(false,false);
                    for mut c in checks{super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut c,&mut |e|match e{
                        Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="now"=>now=true,
                        Expr::Member{base,name,..} if name=="status"&&matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Name{name,..} if aliases.contains(name))=>status=true,_=>{}
                    });}
                    let module = std::path::Path::new(&doc.file).file_stem().and_then(|s| s.to_str()).unwrap_or("schema");
                    let job_name = if module == "schema" { p.name.clone() } else { format!("{module}.{}", p.name) };
                    let already_named = bound.notes.iter().any(|n| n.code == "N_SWEEP_DEADLINE" && n.site == m.site
                        && n.message.contains(&format!("column {table}.status)"))
                        && n.message.contains(&format!("scheduled job {job_name} ")));
                    if status&&!now&&!already_named{note(&mut out,&m.site,"N_SWEEP_ONLY_DEADLINE",format!("{} gates on {table}.status while clock job {} changes that status; writes can run between the deadline and sweep",m.name,p.name),"enforce the deadline against server now in the mutation too; a sweep cleans up state but does not enforce an exact deadline");}
                }}
            }
        }
    }
    out
}
